# Legacy Frontend Git History Audit

Status: authoritative companion to `LEGACY_FRONTEND_FUNCTIONAL_REQUIREMENTS.md`.

## 1. Scope

This audit reviews every Git commit touching the old frontend runtime surfaces `packages/frontend/src`, `packages/frontend/public`, or `packages/frontend/index.html` up to the refactor baseline at `b6ef2955`.

Total reviewed runtime commits: **1033**.

The audit is deliberately not a "restore every historical implementation" list. For each commit the process is:

1. Treat the commit as evidence that a behavior may have existed.
2. Check the final old frontend baseline to determine whether the behavior was still reachable/product-visible.
3. If it survived, map the behavior to an `FR-*` requirement, including edge-case fixes that define stable semantics.
4. If later commits replaced it, use the final behavior rather than reviving an obsolete setting/state machine.
5. Pure test/build/dependency/style changes are evidence only, not standalone requirements.

## 2. Review outcome

- **INTERMEDIATE / BASELINE CHECK**: 602
- **BEHAVIOR REVIEWED**: 370
- **PRESENTATION / BASELINE CHECK**: 16
- **NON-REQUIREMENT**: 44
- **MIXED / SUPERSEDED DETAIL**: 1

## 3. Important history-derived corrections to the requirements

The Git pass found product behavior that was not explicit enough in the first static-code requirement scan. The requirement baseline was updated to include:

- independent connection-data ZIP export;
- Workspace terminal-tab drag ordering and wheel-to-horizontal overflow navigation;
- per-session unsent command-input drafts (already naturally satisfied by one mounted command bar per session in the new runtime);
- File Editor remote refresh, LF/CRLF/CR conversion, and per-tab horizontal/vertical scroll state;
- folder drag/drop upload with relative paths, remote-directory preparation and empty-file support;
- upload ACK/window/backpressure and reconnect invalidation semantics;
- exact Workspace-session binding for downloads;
- symbolic-link content-operation semantics;
- archive dotfile/warning behavior;
- bounded recent-first suspend terminal snapshots;
- RDP/VNC bidirectional plain-text clipboard synchronization;
- PWA/service-worker stale-bundle recovery after deployments.

The pass also rejected historical implementation details that do **not** belong in parity:

- terminal-theme preview actions remain in the final old store but have no final reachable UI caller, so they are dead implementation state rather than a product requirement;
- the earlier `clearFileEditorTabsOnClose` setting was superseded by the later unified popup hide/close cache semantics and must not be reintroduced;
- old WS/SFTP message names, compatibility frames and private Xterm/Monaco internals are implementation details and are explicitly excluded by the clean architecture constraints.

## 4. Design rule for parity fixes

Parity means preserving the useful product behavior, not preserving accidental architecture. When a historical behavior depended on duplicated stores, implicit `undefined/null/empty` conventions, private library APIs, or UI-owned transport state, the new implementation may redesign it as long as the externally useful behavior and edge-case safety remain.

Examples already applied:

- Workspace tab ordering is owned once by the runtime registry; the tab component emits only reorder intent instead of keeping a second draggable-session array.
- per-session command drafts are owned once by `WorkspaceRuntimeSession`; desktop/mobile command-bar presentations bind the same draft, so tab/pane/route presentation changes cannot discard or duplicate it.
- File Editor encoding reload now asks before discarding dirty edits and resets dirty state consistently, fixing the old contradictory "discard content but stay dirty" behavior.

## 4.1 Late-history stability review (2026-07-30 through baseline)

The final development period contains many fixes whose commit titles look implementation-oriented but actually define durable product behavior. These commits were manually rechecked against the final old baseline before updating the FR document.

| Commit                                            | Final behavior retained                                                                                                                                            | Requirement mapping          |
| ------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ---------------------------- |
| `4eec3177`                                        | empty directories retain usable parent navigation                                                                                                                  | `FR-FS-001`                  |
| `9226444d`                                        | File Manager terminal-path commands target the owning Workspace session                                                                                            | `FR-TERM-008`, `FR-ARCH-002` |
| `8a827305` / `617f2099`                           | terminal-directory changes are safely queued/confirmed instead of blindly injecting `cd` during foreground output                                                  | `FR-TERM-008`                |
| `9f6597ce`                                        | terminal right click copies an existing selection; paste is a separate no-selection action and uses xterm paste semantics                                          | `FR-TERM-005`                |
| `1ae6752a`                                        | suspended-session background polling backs off on 429 and repeated errors do not spam duplicate notifications                                                      | `FR-WS-005`                  |
| `317bc856`                                        | suspend terminal snapshot is bounded and recent-output biased                                                                                                      | `FR-WS-005`                  |
| `67ead552`                                        | suspend success is shown only after backend confirmation                                                                                                           | `FR-WS-005`                  |
| `6e9c08a1` / `5e489a7b`                           | editor can force-refresh remote content and convert LF/CRLF/CR                                                                                                     | `FR-EDIT-003`, `FR-EDIT-005` |
| `725c73bd`                                        | download is bound to the exact active Workspace session                                                                                                            | `FR-XFER-009`, `FR-ARCH-002` |
| `8e3c3e05` / `2163d4d3`                           | folder upload prepares the remote tree first, preserves relative paths and accepts empty files                                                                     | `FR-XFER-001A`               |
| `e1ecb5d0`                                        | content operations follow current symbolic-link targets while path mutations stay path-oriented                                                                    | `FR-FS-007`                  |
| `0f1691b2` / `db41df55`                           | archives preserve dotfile names and can finish with warnings/partial outcome rather than corrupting a valid archive                                                | `FR-XFER-008`                |
| `9e6f195e`                                        | desktop file opening is double-click oriented while selection remains a separate click interaction                                                                 | `FR-FS-001`, `FR-FS-003`     |
| `d0b69438` and later upload performance commits   | upload transport may optimize away per-chunk application RTT as long as bounded backpressure, ordered reliable transport and final server-side verification remain | `FR-XFER-002`                |
| `d5ba109a`                                        | desktop editor popup size is browser-local and isolated from mobile fullscreen sizing                                                                              | `FR-EDIT-002`                |
| `2f2d9e0b` / `60a364ab`                           | status history uses stable sequence-anchored buckets; network peaks and scale remain readable                                                                      | `FR-STATUS-001`              |
| `a96f25bd` / `cdb9dbb4` / `fb7eee5f` / `6462fde3` | PDF continuous scroll/page jumps remain correct across hide/show and lazy measurement                                                                              | `FR-PREV-007`                |
| `e283ed0f` / `e96c6a34`                           | desktop PDF outline visibility can persist independently of mobile drawer state                                                                                    | `FR-PREV-007`                |
| `2910951c`                                        | Quick Command copy has a fallback when modern Clipboard API cannot complete the operation                                                                          | `FR-QC-006`                  |
| `dbb68052`                                        | blank CAPTCHA secret fields preserve existing secrets and are cleared from form memory after save                                                                  | `FR-AUTH-006`                |
| `d7bee113`                                        | Telegram notification supports a custom API domain; final notification form also retains Email/Webhook templates and complete channel config                       | `FR-NOTIFY-001`              |
| `5a04a138` / `6ecbbb3c` / `da534e8c`              | remote desktop focus/clipboard/mobile keyboard helpers degrade independently without disconnecting the desktop session                                             | `FR-RD-005`, `FR-RD-006`     |

The upload ACK history is intentionally interpreted semantically rather than literally. The old code moved between per-chunk ACK and progressively larger windows for throughput. The durable requirement is **reliable ordered upload with bounded flow control and authoritative server completion**, not preservation of one ACK mechanism. The refactor may therefore use WebSocket/TCP reliability, browser `bufferedAmount` watermarks and server `pause()/resume()` backpressure without a per-chunk RTT gate.

### Additional final-baseline mappings discovered after the late-history pass

| Commit / evidence                                           | Final behavior retained                                                                                             | Requirement mapping |
| ----------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------- |
| `1b246689` and final `Terminal.vue` / `CommandInputBar.vue` | terminal scrollback search with next/previous/clear and keyboard search mode                                        | `FR-TERM-009`       |
| `cf854364` plus final `useFileManagerDragAndDrop.ts`        | remote rows can be moved by dragging onto directories/parent; selected-set drag moves the selection                 | `FR-FS-009`         |
| final `useFileManagerKeyboardNavigation.ts`                 | wrapped ArrowUp/ArrowDown navigation, parent-entry navigation, Enter activation and scroll-into-view                | `FR-FS-003`         |

These mappings also reinforce the parity-design rule: where the old implementation used a component event bus or duplicated composables, the refactor keeps the behavior but routes it through the existing feature port/controller/runtime intent instead of reproducing the old topology.

| Commit                               | Final behavior retained                                                                                   | Requirement mapping |
| ------------------------------------ | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------- |
| `daf5711e`                           | saved connections include free-form notes through create/edit flows                                       | `FR-CONN-001`       |

The older editor/Workspace implementations used stores and component event buses directly. Those dependencies are not requirements: the refactor keeps the same launch/search/highlighting capabilities through File Editor public controllers and Workspace runtime intents.

| Final evidence                                         | Final behavior retained                                                                                                                        | Requirement mapping                               |
| ------------------------------------------------------ | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------- |
| final `SetupView.vue` + router guards                  | setup is mandatory while required; success initializes the instance but resets auth and returns to Login                                       | `FR-AUTH-001`                                     |

Historical terminal-theme preview state remains excluded: its final store actions have no reachable final Style Customizer caller.

### Final-owner reachability and infrastructure corrections

The final source tree still contains several view files that are not routes in the final router (`TagsView`, `QuickCommandsView`, `CommandHistoryView`, `SuspendedSshSessionsView`). Their underlying feature behaviors remain requirements where they are reachable through Workspace/layout/modal surfaces, but a standalone page/route is **not** inferred merely from a leftover file.

| Final evidence / history                               | Final behavior retained                                                                                              | Requirement mapping |
| ------------------------------------------------------ | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------- |
| `1ae6752a` + final `uiNotifications.store.ts`          | short-lived global feedback with duplicate-error suppression for noisy background failures                           | `FR-FEEDBACK-001`   |
| final `layout.store.ts` / `LayoutConfigurator.vue`     | backend→local-cache→default layout fallback, validation, recursive editing, sidebars and atomic save/reset semantics | `FR-WS-006`         |
| final `AuditLogView.vue` / audit store                 | explicit apply-filter resets page, page navigation retains filters, readable raw/JSON details                        | `FR-AUDIT-001`      |
| final `TagInput.vue` / `ManageTagConnectionsModal.vue` | embedded searchable tag selection/creation and group membership management; no standalone final route requirement    | `FR-TAG-001`        |
| final `AddProxyForm.vue`                               | SOCKS5/HTTP proxy validation plus preserve-versus-explicit-clear password semantics                                  | `FR-PROXY-001`      |
| final `SshKeyManagementModal.vue`                      | add/edit/delete keys without pre-filling secret material; blank edit preserves key/passphrase                        | `FR-SSHKEY-001`     |

The refactor should improve the old infrastructure where possible. In particular, it does not need to preserve the legacy dialog Pinia store's duplicated ref/state representation or the legacy SSH-key edit call that fetched decrypted details only to blank the secret fields again.

Notes:

- `stores/session*` is explicitly treated as implementation evidence across Workspace/Terminal/Filesystem/Editor/Remote Desktop. The old mega-store is **not** a parity requirement.
- Final view files with no final route (`TagsView`, `QuickCommandsView`, `CommandHistoryView`, `SuspendedSshSessionsView`) map to their reachable feature behavior only; no standalone route is implied.
- Generic device detection maps to mobile/architecture requirements and remains a reusable browser capability, not a product-state owner.

## 4.3 Explicit non-requirement / superseded-state list

The following final/historical implementation artifacts were deliberately reviewed and **excluded** from parity so future refactors do not accidentally revive dead design:

- `layout.store.isLayoutVisible` / `toggleLayoutVisibility`: final store state/action has no final product caller. It is not the same as the real `isHeaderVisible` feature. Workspace top-header visibility **is** required and maps to `FR-SET-004` / `FR-WS-006`.
- Appearance `isPreviewingTerminalTheme` / `previewTerminalThemeData`: final store state/actions have no reachable final Style Customizer caller. Do not add a terminal-theme Preview workflow solely because the store still contains it.
- `clearFileEditorTabsOnClose`: superseded before the final baseline by the explicit Preview/File Editor popup hide-vs-close cache semantics. Do not restore the old setting.
- `TagsView.vue`: the final router leaves `/tags` commented out. Tag creation/selection/group management remains required through reachable embedded surfaces, but a standalone Tags route does not.
- `QuickCommandsView.vue`, `CommandHistoryView.vue`, `SuspendedSshSessionsView.vue`: these are reachable as embedded/layout/modal content but not final standalone routes. Preserve their feature behavior, not nonexistent direct routes.
- `stores/session*`: the legacy mega-store is implementation topology, not a requirement. Its behaviors are covered by Workspace/Terminal/Filesystem/Editor/Remote Desktop FRs and should remain split by owner in the refactor.
- legacy `ssh:*`, `sftp:*`, `SSH_*`, NXTM/NXUP messages and snake_case transport DTOs: historical protocol implementation, explicitly not part of product parity.
- private Xterm/Monaco implementation access: not a requirement; public APIs are preferred and the current refactor intentionally keeps `_core` usage at zero.
